import random
from datetime import timedelta

from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import Profile
from apps.appointments.models import Appointment
from apps.billing.models import MedicalAct
from apps.clinical.models import Consultation, ConsultationAct
from apps.patients.models import Patient

ACTS_CATALOG = [
    ("CONS_GYN", "Consultation gynÃ©cologique", 300, False),
    ("ECHO_OBST", "Ã‰chographie obstÃ©tricale", 500, False),
    ("ECHO_PELV", "Ã‰chographie pelvienne", 400, False),
    ("FROTTIS", "Frottis cervico-vaginal", 250, True),
    ("STERILET", "Pose stÃ©rilet", 450, False),
    ("PRELEV_BACT", "PrÃ©lÃ¨vement bactÃ©riologique", 150, True),
    ("POSE_SONDE", "Pose de sonde vÃ©sicale", 120, True),
]

PATIENTS_DATA = [
    ("Fatima", "El Amrani", "AB123456", "0661020304"),
    ("Khadija", "Bennani", "AB234567", "0662030405"),
    ("Salma", "Idrissi", "AB345678", "0663040506"),
    ("Nadia", "Chraibi", "AB456789", "0664050607"),
    ("Meryem", "Tazi", "AB567890", "0665060708"),
    ("Amina", "Fassi", "AB678901", "0666070809"),
    ("Hanane", "Alaoui", "AB789012", "0667080910"),
    ("Ilham", "Berrada", "AB890123", "0668091011"),
    ("Zineb", "Skalli", "AB901234", "0669101112"),
    ("Rajae", "Ouazzani", "AB012345", "0660111213"),
]

APPOINTMENT_STATUSES = [
    Appointment.Status.SCHEDULED,
    Appointment.Status.ARRIVED,
    Appointment.Status.WAITING,
    Appointment.Status.IN_CONSULTATION,
    Appointment.Status.COMPLETED,
]


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Seed demo data: users, medical acts catalog, patients, today's appointments and consultations."

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        doctor = self._create_user("dr_errmili", "errmili2026", Profile.Role.DOCTOR, "Khadija", "Errmili")
        self._create_user("assistante", "assistante2026", Profile.Role.ASSISTANT, "Assistante", "Cabinet")

        acts = {}
        for code, name, price, is_quick_exam in ACTS_CATALOG:
            act, _ = MedicalAct.objects.update_or_create(
                code=code,
                defaults={
                    "name": name,
                    "price": price,
                    "is_active": True,
                    "is_quick_exam": is_quick_exam,
                },
            )
            acts[code] = act
        self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS(f"{len(acts)} actes mÃ©dicaux dans le catalogue."))

        today = timezone.localdate()
        base_hour = 9
        created_patients = 0
        created_appointments = 0
        created_consultations = 0

        for i, (first_name, last_name, cin, phone) in enumerate(PATIENTS_DATA):
            patient, was_created = Patient.objects.get_or_create(
                cin=cin,
                defaults={
                    "first_name": first_name,
                    "last_name": last_name,
                    "phone": phone,
                    "emergency_contact": f"{phone[:4]} (famille)",
                    "allergies": "" if i % 3 else "PÃ©nicilline",
                    "medical_history": "RAS" if i % 2 else "Suivi grossesse antÃ©rieure",
                },
            )
            if was_created:
                created_patients += 1

            status = APPOINTMENT_STATUSES[i % len(APPOINTMENT_STATUSES)]
            appt_time = timezone.make_aware(
                timezone.datetime.combine(today, timezone.datetime.min.time())
                + timedelta(hours=base_hour, minutes=30 * i)
            )
            appointment, appt_created = Appointment.objects.get_or_create(
                patient=patient,
                date=appt_time,
                defaults={
                    "status": status,
                    "reason": random.choice(["Consultation de routine", "Suivi grossesse", "ContrÃ´le"]),
                    "whatsapp_status": Appointment.WhatsappStatus.CONFIRMED
                    if status != Appointment.Status.SCHEDULED
                    else Appointment.WhatsappStatus.PENDING,
                },
            )
            if appt_created:
                created_appointments += 1

            if status in (Appointment.Status.IN_CONSULTATION, Appointment.Status.COMPLETED):
                consultation, consult_created = Consultation.objects.get_or_create(
                    appointment=appointment,
                    defaults={
                        "patient": patient,
                        "doctor": doctor,
                        "clinical_notes": "Examen clinique sans particularitÃ©.",
                        "diagnostic": "Suivi gynÃ©cologique de routine.",
                    },
                )
                if consult_created:
                    created_consultations += 1
                    chosen_codes = random.sample(list(acts.keys()), k=random.choice([1, 2]))
                    for code in chosen_codes:
                        act = acts[code]
                        ConsultationAct.objects.get_or_create(
                            consultation=consultation, act=act, defaults={"price": act.price}
                        )
                    if status == Appointment.Status.COMPLETED:
                        consultation.status = Consultation.Status.VALIDATED
                        consultation.save()

        self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS(f"{created_patients} nouvelles patientes crÃ©Ã©es."))
        self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS(f"{created_appointments} nouveaux rendez-vous crÃ©Ã©s."))
        self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS(f"{created_consultations} nouvelles consultations crÃ©Ã©es."))
        self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS("Seed terminÃ©."))

    @staticmethod
    def _create_user(username, password, role, first_name, last_name):
        user, created = User.objects.get_or_create(
            username=username,
            defaults={"first_name": first_name, "last_name": last_name},
        )
        if created:
            user.set_password(password)
            user.save()
        Profile.objects.update_or_create(user=user, defaults={"role": role})
        return user

========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\apps\accounts\admin.py ==========

from django.contrib import admin

from .models import Profile


class TenantScopedAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    """Restreint les non-superusers aux objets de leur propre cabinet."""

    def get_queryset(self, request):
        qs = super().get_queryset(request)
        if request.user.is_superuser:
            return qs
        profile = getattr(request.user, "profile", None)
        if profile is None:
            return qs.none()
        return qs.filter(cabinet=profile.cabinet)


@admin.register(Profile)
class ProfileAdmin(TenantScopedAdmin):
    list_display = ("user", "role", "cabinet")
    list_filter = ("role", "cabinet")
    search_fields = ("user__username", "user__first_name", "user__last_name")

========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\apps\accounts\decorators.py ==========

from functools import wraps

from django.http import HttpResponseForbidden


def role_required(*roles):
    """Restrict a view to users whose profile.role is one of `roles`."""

    def decorator(view_func):
        @wraps(view_func)
        def wrapped(request, *args, **kwargs):
            profile = getattr(request.user, "profile", None)
            if not request.user.is_authenticated or profile is None or profile.role not in roles:
                return HttpResponseForbidden("AccÃ¨s refusÃ© : rÃ´le insuffisant.")
            return view_func(request, *args, **kwargs)

        return wrapped

    return decorator


========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\apps\accounts\middleware.py ==========

from django.conf import settings
from django.http import HttpResponseForbidden


class ClinicalAccessMiddleware:
    """Unconditionally blocks the 'assistant' role from any clinical URL.

    This is a safety net independent of individual views' @role_required
    decorators: even a clinical view that forgets to decorate itself stays
    inaccessible to an assistant.
    """

    def __init__(self, get_response):
        self.get_response = get_response
        self.prefixes = tuple(getattr(settings, "CLINICAL_URL_PREFIXES", ["/clinical/"]))

    def __call__(self, request):
        if request.path.startswith(self.prefixes):
            profile = getattr(getattr(request, "user", None), "profile", None)
            if profile is not None and profile.role == "assistant":
                return HttpResponseForbidden("AccÃ¨s refusÃ© : zone clinique rÃ©servÃ©e au mÃ©decin.")
        return self.get_response(request)


========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\apps\accounts\models.py ==========

from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models


class Cabinet(models.Model):
    nom = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    slug = models.SlugField(max_length=160, unique=True)
    initiales_logo = models.CharField(max_length=4, blank=True)
    actif = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Cabinet"
        verbose_name_plural = "Cabinets"
        ordering = ["nom"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.nom


class Profile(models.Model):
    class Role(models.TextChoices):
        DOCTOR = "doctor", "MÃ©decin"
        ASSISTANT = "assistant", "Assistante"

    user = models.OneToOneField(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="profile"
    )
    cabinet = models.ForeignKey(
        Cabinet, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="profiles", null=True, blank=True
    )
    role = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=Role.choices, default=Role.ASSISTANT)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Profil"
        verbose_name_plural = "Profils"

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.get_username()} ({self.get_role_display()})"

    @property
    def is_doctor(self):
        return self.role == self.Role.DOCTOR

    @property
    def is_assistant(self):
        return self.role == self.Role.ASSISTANT

from django.conf import settings
from django.db.models.signals import post_save
from django.dispatch import receiver

from .models import Profile


@receiver(post_save, sender=settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL)
def create_profile_for_new_user(sender, instance, created, **kwargs):
    if created:
        Profile.objects.get_or_create(user=instance)

========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\apps\accounts\views.py ==========

from django.contrib.auth import logout
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.contrib.auth.views import LoginView, LogoutView
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render
from django.utils.text import slugify

from .models import Cabinet, Profile


def tenant_discovery(request):
    error = None
    if request.method == "POST":
        raw_slug = request.POST.get("slug", "").strip()
        cabinet = Cabinet.objects.filter(slug=slugify(raw_slug), actif=True).first()
        if cabinet:
            return redirect("tenant_login", slug=cabinet.slug)
        error = "Espace introuvable."
    return render(request, "accounts/discovery.html", {"error": error})


class TenantLoginView(LoginView):
    template_name = "accounts/login.html"

    def dispatch(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        self.cabinet = get_object_or_404(Cabinet, slug=kwargs["slug"], actif=True)
        return super().dispatch(request, *args, **kwargs)

    def get_context_data(self, **kwargs):
        context = super().get_context_data(**kwargs)
        context["cabinet"] = self.cabinet
        return context

    def form_valid(self, form):
        response = super().form_valid(form)
        profile = getattr(form.get_user(), "profile", None)
        if not profile or profile.cabinet_id != self.cabinet.id:
            logout(self.request)
            form.add_error(None, "Vous n'appartenez pas Ã  ce cabinet.")
            return self.form_invalid(form)
        return response

    def get_success_url(self):
        return "/accounts/redirect/"


tenant_login = TenantLoginView.as_view()


class AccountLogoutView(LogoutView):
    next_page = "accounts:login"


@login_required
def post_login_redirect(request):
    profile = getattr(request.user, "profile", None)
    if profile and profile.role == Profile.Role.DOCTOR:
        return redirect("clinical:doctor_dashboard")
    return redirect("appointments:assistant_dashboard")

========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\apps\appointments\management\commands\seed_september_appointments.py ==========

import calendar
import random
from datetime import date, time

from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import Cabinet
from apps.appointments.models import Appointment
from apps.patients.models import Patient

DAY_START_MINUTES = 8 * 60 + 30
DAY_END_MINUTES = 18 * 60 + 30
SLOT_MINUTES = 30

REASONS = [
    "Consultation de suivi",
    "Ã‰chographie",
    "ContrÃ´le",
    "PremiÃ¨re consultation",
    "Suivi grossesse",
    "Consultation de routine",
]

STATUS_POOL = [
    Appointment.Status.SCHEDULED,
    Appointment.Status.SCHEDULED,
    Appointment.Status.COMPLETED,
    Appointment.Status.COMPLETED,
    Appointment.Status.CANCELLED,
]


def _day_slots():
    slots = []
    minutes = DAY_START_MINUTES
    while minutes <= DAY_END_MINUTES - SLOT_MINUTES:
        slots.append(time(minutes // 60, minutes % 60))
        minutes += SLOT_MINUTES
    return slots


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "CrÃ©e des rendez-vous fictifs sur tout le mois de septembre (3 Ã  5 par jour) pour les tests."

    def add_arguments(self, parser):
        parser.add_argument("--year", type=int, default=None, help="AnnÃ©e cible (dÃ©faut: annÃ©e courante).")
        parser.add_argument(
            "--skip-sundays",
            action="store_true",
            default=True,
            help="N'ajoute pas de rendez-vous le dimanche (activÃ© par dÃ©faut).",
        )

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        cabinet = Cabinet.objects.first()
        if not cabinet:
            self.stderr.write(self.style.ERROR("Aucun cabinet trouvÃ© â€” lancez d'abord seed_demo_data."))
            return

        patients = list(Patient.objects.filter(cabinet=cabinet))
        if not patients:
            self.stderr.write(self.style.ERROR("Aucune patiente trouvÃ©e â€” lancez d'abord seed_demo_data."))
            return

        year = options["year"] or timezone.localdate().year
        month = 9
        days_in_month = calendar.monthrange(year, month)[1]
        slots = _day_slots()

        created_count = 0
        for day in range(1, days_in_month + 1):
            current_date = date(year, month, day)
            if options["skip_sundays"] and current_date.weekday() == 6:
                continue

            nb_appointments = random.randint(3, 5)
            day_slots = random.sample(slots, k=min(nb_appointments, len(slots)))
            day_patients = random.sample(patients, k=min(nb_appointments, len(patients)))

            for slot_time, patient in zip(sorted(day_slots), day_patients):
                appt_dt = timezone.make_aware(
                    timezone.datetime.combine(current_date, slot_time)
                )
                status = random.choice(STATUS_POOL)
                _, was_created = Appointment.objects.get_or_create(
                    cabinet=cabinet,
                    patient=patient,
                    date=appt_dt,
                    defaults={
                        "status": status,
                        "reason": random.choice(REASONS),
                        "whatsapp_status": Appointment.WhatsappStatus.PENDING,
                    },
                )
                if was_created:
                    created_count += 1

        self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS(f"{created_count} rendez-vous fictifs crÃ©Ã©s pour septembre {year}."))

========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\apps\appointments\models.py ==========

from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import Cabinet
from apps.common.whatsapp import appointment_reminder_link
from apps.patients.models import Patient


class Appointment(models.Model):
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        SCHEDULED = "scheduled", "PlanifiÃ©"
        ARRIVED = "arrived", "ArrivÃ©e"
        WAITING = "waiting", "En attente"
        IN_CONSULTATION = "in_consultation", "En consultation"
        COMPLETED = "completed", "TerminÃ©"
        CANCELLED = "cancelled", "AnnulÃ©"

    class WhatsappStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = "pending", "En attente"
        SENT = "sent", "EnvoyÃ©"
        CONFIRMED = "confirmed", "ConfirmÃ©"
        RESCHEDULED = "rescheduled", "ReprogrammÃ©"
        CANCELLED = "cancelled", "AnnulÃ©"

    cabinet = models.ForeignKey(
        Cabinet, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="appointments", null=True, blank=True
    )
    patient = models.ForeignKey(
        Patient, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="appointments"
    )
    date = models.DateTimeField()
    duration_minutes = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=30)
    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.SCHEDULED
    )
    reason = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)
    whatsapp_status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=WhatsappStatus.choices, default=WhatsappStatus.PENDING
    )
    admin_notes = models.TextField(blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Rendez-vous"
        verbose_name_plural = "Rendez-vous"
        ordering = ["date"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.patient} â€” {self.date:%d/%m/%Y %H:%M}"

    @property
    def whatsapp_reminder_link(self):
        local_dt = timezone.localtime(self.date)
        return appointment_reminder_link(
            self.patient, local_dt.strftime("%d/%m/%Y"), local_dt.strftime("%H:%M")
        )

import calendar as calendar_module
import json
from datetime import date, datetime, time, timedelta

from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.db.models import Q, Sum
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import Profile
from apps.billing.models import Invoice
from apps.clinical.models import Consultation
from apps.communications.views import _active_tasks_for
from apps.patients.models import Patient

from .models import Appointment

NEXT_STATUS = {
    Appointment.Status.SCHEDULED: Appointment.Status.ARRIVED,
    Appointment.Status.ARRIVED: Appointment.Status.WAITING,
    Appointment.Status.WAITING: Appointment.Status.IN_CONSULTATION,
    Appointment.Status.IN_CONSULTATION: Appointment.Status.COMPLETED,
}

NEXT_STATUS_LABEL = {
    Appointment.Status.SCHEDULED: "Marquer arrivÃ©e",
    Appointment.Status.ARRIVED: "Mettre en attente",
    Appointment.Status.WAITING: "DÃ©marrer consultation",
    Appointment.Status.IN_CONSULTATION: "Marquer terminÃ©e",
}

QUEUE_COLUMNS = [
    (Appointment.Status.ARRIVED, "ArrivÃ©e"),
    (Appointment.Status.WAITING, "En attente"),
    (Appointment.Status.IN_CONSULTATION, "En consultation"),
    (Appointment.Status.COMPLETED, "TerminÃ©e"),
]

DAY_START = time(8, 30)
DAY_END = time(18, 30)
SLOT_MINUTES = 30
DAY_START_MINUTES = DAY_START.hour * 60 + DAY_START.minute
DAY_END_MINUTES = DAY_END.hour * 60 + DAY_END.minute
DAY_TOTAL_MINUTES = DAY_END_MINUTES - DAY_START_MINUTES

REASON_PRESETS = [
    "Consultation de suivi",
    "Ã‰chographie",
    "ContrÃ´le",
    "PremiÃ¨re consultation",
    "Urgence",
]

STATUS_FILTERS = [
    ("all", "Tous"),
    (Appointment.Status.SCHEDULED, "ProgrammÃ©s"),
    (Appointment.Status.ARRIVED, "ArrivÃ©s"),
    (Appointment.Status.WAITING, "En attente"),
    (Appointment.Status.IN_CONSULTATION, "En consultation"),
    (Appointment.Status.COMPLETED, "TerminÃ©es"),
]


def _toast_header(message, toast_type="success"):
    return json.dumps({"toast": {"message": message, "type": toast_type}})


def _cabinet_doctor(cabinet):
    return User.objects.filter(
        profile__role=Profile.Role.DOCTOR, profile__cabinet=cabinet
    ).first()


def _today_appointments(cabinet):
    today = timezone.localdate()
    return (
        Appointment.objects.select_related("patient")
        .filter(cabinet=cabinet, date__date=today)
        .exclude(status=Appointment.Status.CANCELLED)
        .order_by("date")
    )


def _queue_board_context(cabinet):
    appointments = _today_appointments(cabinet)
    for appointment in appointments:
        appointment.next_label = NEXT_STATUS_LABEL.get(appointment.status)
    columns = []
    for status, label in QUEUE_COLUMNS:
        columns.append((status, label, [a for a in appointments if a.status == status]))
    return {"columns": columns}


def _today_list_context(cabinet):
    appointments = _today_appointments(cabinet)
    scheduled_today = [a for a in appointments if a.status == Appointment.Status.SCHEDULED]
    return {"scheduled_today": scheduled_today}


def _j2_context(cabinet):
    target_date = timezone.localdate() + timezone.timedelta(days=2)
    appointments = (
        Appointment.objects.select_related("patient")
        .filter(cabinet=cabinet, date__date=target_date)
        .exclude(status=Appointment.Status.CANCELLED)
        .order_by("date")
    )
    return {"j2_appointments": appointments, "j2_date": target_date}


def _parse_date(value):
    if not value:
        return None
    try:
        return datetime.strptime(value, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
    except ValueError:
        return None


def _shift_month(anchor, delta):
    month_index = anchor.month - 1 + delta
    year = anchor.year + month_index // 12
    month = month_index % 12 + 1
    day = min(anchor.day, calendar_module.monthrange(year, month)[1])
    return date(year, month, day)


def _day_slots():
    slots = []
    minutes = DAY_START_MINUTES
    while minutes <= DAY_END_MINUTES:
        slots.append(time(minutes // 60, minutes % 60))
        minutes += SLOT_MINUTES
    return slots


def _day_context(cabinet, current_date, status_filter="all"):
    appointments = (
        Appointment.objects.select_related("patient")
        .filter(cabinet=cabinet, date__date=current_date)
        .order_by("date")
    )
    if status_filter and status_filter != "all":
        appointments = appointments.filter(status=status_filter)
    positioned = []
    for appt in appointments:
        local_dt = timezone.localtime(appt.date)
        start_minutes = local_dt.hour * 60 + local_dt.minute
        offset = start_minutes - DAY_START_MINUTES
        top_pct = min(max((offset / DAY_TOTAL_MINUTES) * 100, 0), 100)
        height_pct = max((appt.duration_minutes / DAY_TOTAL_MINUTES) * 100, 3.2)
        appt.top_pct = round(top_pct, 2)
        appt.height_pct = round(height_pct, 2)
        appt.next_label = NEXT_STATUS_LABEL.get(appt.status)
        appt.time_display = local_dt.strftime("%H:%M")
        appt.date_iso = local_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M")
        positioned.append(appt)

    now_pct = None
    if current_date == timezone.localdate():
        now_local = timezone.localtime(timezone.now())
        now_minutes = now_local.hour * 60 + now_local.minute
        if DAY_START_MINUTES <= now_minutes <= DAY_END_MINUTES:
            now_pct = round(((now_minutes - DAY_START_MINUTES) / DAY_TOTAL_MINUTES) * 100, 2)

    return {
        "slots": _day_slots(),
        "day_appointments": positioned,
        "now_pct": now_pct,
    }


def _month_context(cabinet, current_date):
    cal = calendar_module.Calendar(firstweekday=0)
    weeks = list(cal.monthdatescalendar(current_date.year, current_date.month))

    month_appointments = (
        Appointment.objects.select_related("patient")
        .filter(cabinet=cabinet, date__date__gte=weeks[0][0], date__date__lte=weeks[-1][-1])
        .exclude(status=Appointment.Status.CANCELLED)
        .order_by("date")
    )
    by_day = {}
    for appt in month_appointments:
        by_day.setdefault(timezone.localtime(appt.date).date(), []).append(appt)

    today = timezone.localdate()
    grid = []
    for week in weeks:
        row = []
        for day in week:
            day_appts = by_day.get(day, [])
            count = len(day_appts)
            if count == 0:
                load = "none"
            elif count <= 4:
                load = "normal"
            elif count <= 8:
                load = "busy"
            else:
                load = "full"
            row.append(
                {
                    "date": day,
                    "in_month": day.month == current_date.month,
                    "is_today": day == today,
                    "appointments": day_appts[:3],
                    "extra_count": max(count - 3, 0),
                    "total": count,
                    "load": load,
                }
            )
        grid.append(row)

    return {"month_grid": grid}


def _calendar_extra_context(cabinet, current_date):
    return {
        "today": timezone.localdate(),
        "prev_date": current_date - timedelta(days=1),
        "next_date": current_date + timedelta(days=1),
        "prev_month": _shift_month(current_date, -1),
        "next_month": _shift_month(current_date, 1),
        "reason_presets": REASON_PRESETS,
        "status_filters": STATUS_FILTERS,
        **_month_context(cabinet, current_date),
    }


@login_required
def appointment_calendar(request):
    view_mode = request.GET.get("view", "day")
    if view_mode not in ("day", "month"):
        view_mode = "day"
    current_date = _parse_date(request.GET.get("date")) or timezone.localdate()
    status_filter = request.GET.get("status", "all")

    cabinet = request.user.profile.cabinet
    context = {
        "view_mode": view_mode,
        "current_date": current_date,
        "status_filter": status_filter,
        **_calendar_extra_context(cabinet, current_date),
    }
    if view_mode == "day":
        context.update(_day_context(cabinet, current_date, status_filter))

    if request.headers.get("HX-Request"):
        response = render(request, "assistant/_calendar_container.html", context)
        response.headers["HX-Push-Url"] = (
            f"{request.path}?view={view_mode}&date={current_date.isoformat()}&status={status_filter}"
        )
        return response
    return render(request, "assistant/calendar.html", context)


def _render_day_fragment(request, anchor_date, toast_message=None):
    cabinet = request.user.profile.cabinet
    context = {
        "view_mode": "day",
        "current_date": anchor_date,
        "status_filter": "all",
        **_calendar_extra_context(cabinet, anchor_date),
        **_day_context(cabinet, anchor_date),
    }
    response = render(request, "assistant/_calendar_body.html", context)
    events = {"refreshCalendar": True}
    if toast_message:
        events["toast"] = {"message": toast_message, "type": "success"}
    response.headers["HX-Trigger"] = json.dumps(events)
    response.headers["HX-Push-Url"] = (
        f"{reverse('appointments:calendar')}?view=day&date={anchor_date.isoformat()}"
    )
    return response

    )

@login_required
def save_intake_vitals(request, pk):
    """POST: saves Tension/Poids/Taille/DDR onto the appointment's Consultation.

    Only ever touches these four intake fields â€” never clinical_notes,
    diagnostic, or ultrasound_notes â€” so this endpoint stays safe to expose
    to the assistant role regardless of caller.
    """
    cabinet = request.user.profile.cabinet
    appointment = get_object_or_404(Appointment, pk=pk, cabinet=cabinet)
    consultation = Consultation.objects.filter(appointment=appointment).first()
    error_message = None

    if request.method == "POST":
        if consultation is None:
            doctor = _cabinet_doctor(cabinet)
            if doctor is None:
                error_message = (
                    "Aucun mÃ©decin n'est rattachÃ© Ã  ce cabinet : "
                    "impossible d'enregistrer les constantes."
                )
            else:
                consultation = Consultation.objects.create(
                    appointment=appointment,
                    patient=appointment.patient,
                    cabinet=cabinet,
                    doctor=doctor,
                )

        if consultation is not None:
            consultation.blood_pressure = request.POST.get("blood_pressure", "")
            consultation.weight_kg = request.POST.get("weight_kg") or None
            consultation.height_cm = request.POST.get("height_cm") or None
            consultation.last_menstrual_period = (
                request.POST.get("last_menstrual_period") or None
            )
            consultation.save(
                update_fields=[
                    "blood_pressure",
                    "weight_kg",
                    "height_cm",
                    "last_menstrual_period",
                ]
            )

    response = render(
        request,
        "assistant/_intake_vitals_form.html",
        {"appointment": appointment, "consultation": consultation},
    )
    if error_message:
        response.headers["HX-Trigger"] = _toast_header(error_message, "error")
    elif consultation is not None:
        response.headers["HX-Trigger"] = _toast_header(
            f"{appointment.patient} â€” constantes enregistrÃ©es."
        )
    return response


@login_required
def create_appointment_ajax(request):
    """POST: creates a RDV from patiente_id/date/heure/motif and refreshes the day grid."""
    if request.method != "POST":
        return redirect("appointments:calendar")

    anchor_date = timezone.localdate()
    patient_id = request.POST.get("patient")
    date_str = request.POST.get("date")
    time_str = request.POST.get("time")
    toast_message = None

    if patient_id and date_str and time_str:
        patient = get_object_or_404(
            Patient, pk=patient_id, cabinet=request.user.profile.cabinet
        )
        naive_dt = datetime.strptime(f"{date_str} {time_str}", "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
        parsed_date = timezone.make_aware(naive_dt)
        Appointment.objects.create(
            cabinet=request.user.profile.cabinet,
            patient=patient,
            date=parsed_date,
            duration_minutes=request.POST.get("duration_minutes") or 30,
            reason=request.POST.get("reason", "").strip(),
            admin_notes=request.POST.get("admin_notes", "").strip(),
        )
        anchor_date = parsed_date.date()
        toast_message = f"RDV crÃ©Ã© pour {patient}."

    return _render_day_fragment(request, anchor_date, toast_message)


@login_required
def update_appointment_ajax(request, pk):
    """POST: reschedules a RDV to a new date/heure and refreshes the day grid."""
    appointment = get_object_or_404(
        Appointment, pk=pk, cabinet=request.user.profile.cabinet
    )
    anchor_date = timezone.localtime(appointment.date).date()
    toast_message = None

    if request.method == "POST":
        date_str = request.POST.get("date")
        time_str = request.POST.get("time")
        if date_str and time_str:
            naive_dt = datetime.strptime(f"{date_str} {time_str}", "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
            parsed_date = timezone.make_aware(naive_dt)
            appointment.date = parsed_date
            appointment.save(update_fields=["date"])
            anchor_date = parsed_date.date()
            toast_message = (
                f"{appointment.patient} â€” RDV reprogrammÃ© au {anchor_date:%d/%m/%Y} Ã  {time_str}."
            )

    return _render_day_fragment(request, anchor_date, toast_message)


@login_required
def cancel_appointment_ajax(request, pk):
    """POST: sets a RDV status to 'Cancelled' and refreshes the day grid."""
    appointment = get_object_or_404(
        Appointment, pk=pk, cabinet=request.user.profile.cabinet
    )
    toast_message = None
    anchor_date = timezone.localtime(appointment.date).date()

    if request.method == "POST":
        appointment.status = Appointment.Status.CANCELLED
        appointment.save(update_fields=["status"])
        toast_message = f"{appointment.patient} â€” RDV annulÃ©."

    return _render_day_fragment(request, anchor_date, toast_message)

    patients = []
    if query:

            Q(first_name__icontains=query)
            | Q(last_name__icontains=query)
            | Q(phone__icontains=query)
            | Q(cin__icontains=query)
            | Q(patient_number__icontains=query),

@login_required
def quick_create_patient(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        first_name = request.POST.get("first_name", "").strip()
        last_name = request.POST.get("last_name", "").strip()
        if first_name and last_name:
            patient = Patient.objects.create(
                cabinet=request.user.profile.cabinet,
                first_name=first_name,
                last_name=last_name,
                phone=request.POST.get("phone", "").strip(),
            )
            return render(
                request, "assistant/_patient_picker.html", {"selected_patient": patient}
            )
    return render(request, "assistant/_patient_picker.html", {"express_error": True})


@login_required
def assistant_dashboard(request):
    cabinet = request.user.profile.cabinet
    pending_invoices = Invoice.objects.select_related("patient").filter(
        status=Invoice.Status.PENDING, cabinet=cabinet
    )
    my_tasks = _active_tasks_for(request.user)
    context = {
        **_queue_board_context(cabinet),
        **_today_list_context(cabinet),
        **_j2_context(cabinet),
        "pending_invoices": pending_invoices,
        "pending_invoices_total": pending_invoices.aggregate(total=Sum("total_amount"))["total"] or 0,
        "my_tasks": my_tasks,
        "urgent_tasks_count": sum(1 for t in my_tasks if t.priority == "urgent"),
        "today_total": _today_appointments(cabinet).count(),
    }
    return render(request, "assistant/dashboard.html", context)


@login_required
def create_appointment(request, patient_id=None):
    cabinet = request.user.profile.cabinet
    patient = (
        get_object_or_404(Patient, pk=patient_id, cabinet=cabinet) if patient_id else None
    )
    if request.method == "POST":
        patient_pk = patient_id or request.POST.get("patient")
        patient = get_object_or_404(Patient, pk=patient_pk, cabinet=cabinet)
        date = request.POST.get("date")
        if date:
            parsed_date = datetime.fromisoformat(date)
            if timezone.is_naive(parsed_date):
                parsed_date = timezone.make_aware(parsed_date)
            Appointment.objects.create(
                cabinet=cabinet,
                patient=patient,
                date=parsed_date,
                duration_minutes=request.POST.get("duration_minutes") or 30,
                reason=request.POST.get("reason", "").strip(),
            )
            messages.success(request, f"RDV crÃ©Ã© pour {patient}.")
            return redirect("appointments:assistant_dashboard")
        messages.error(request, "La date du RDV est obligatoire.")

    patients = Patient.objects.filter(cabinet=cabinet)[:100] if not patient else None
    return render(
        request, "appointments/create.html", {"patient": patient, "patients": patients}
    )

        )[:15]
    return render(request, "assistant/_search_results.html", {"patients": patients, "query": query})

    appointment = get_object_or_404(Appointment, pk=pk, cabinet=cabinet)

    if request.method == "POST":

    response = render(request, "assistant/_queue_board.html", _queue_board_context(cabinet))
    if toast_message:
        response.headers["HX-Trigger"] = _toast_header(toast_message)

WHATSAPP_ACTIONS = {
    "send": (Appointment.WhatsappStatus.SENT, "Rappel WhatsApp J-2 envoyÃ© (simulation) Ã  {patient}."),
    "confirm": (Appointment.WhatsappStatus.CONFIRMED, "{patient} a confirmÃ© son RDV (simulation)."),
    "reschedule": (Appointment.WhatsappStatus.RESCHEDULED, "{patient} souhaite dÃ©placer son RDV (simulation)."),
    "cancel": (Appointment.WhatsappStatus.CANCELLED, "{patient} a annulÃ© son RDV (simulation)."),
}


@login_required
def whatsapp_action(request, pk, action):
    cabinet = request.user.profile.cabinet
    appointment = get_object_or_404(Appointment, pk=pk, cabinet=cabinet)
    toast_message = None
    if request.method == "POST" and action in WHATSAPP_ACTIONS:
        new_status, message_template = WHATSAPP_ACTIONS[action]
        appointment.whatsapp_status = new_status
        appointment.save(update_fields=["whatsapp_status"])
        toast_message = message_template.format(patient=appointment.patient)

    response = render(request, "assistant/_j2_reminders.html", _j2_context(cabinet))
    if toast_message:
        response.headers["HX-Trigger"] = _toast_header(toast_message)
    return response

========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\apps\billing\models.py ==========

from django.db import models
from django.db.models import Max
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import Cabinet
from apps.patients.models import Patient


class MedicalAct(models.Model):
    code = models.CharField(max_length=30, unique=True)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    is_quick_exam = models.BooleanField(
        "Examen complÃ©mentaire rapide", default=False
    )

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Acte mÃ©dical"
        verbose_name_plural = "Actes mÃ©dicaux"
        ordering = ["name"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.code} â€” {self.name}"


class Invoice(models.Model):
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = "pending", "En attente"
        PAID = "paid", "PayÃ©e"
        CANCELLED = "cancelled", "AnnulÃ©e"

    class PaymentMethod(models.TextChoices):
        CASH = "cash", "EspÃ¨ces"
        CARD = "card", "Carte"
        CHECK = "check", "ChÃ¨que"

    cabinet = models.ForeignKey(
        Cabinet, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="invoices", null=True, blank=True
    )
    invoice_number = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True, editable=False)
    patient = models.ForeignKey(
        Patient, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="invoices"
    )
    total_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.PENDING
    )
    payment_method = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=PaymentMethod.choices, blank=True
    )
    paid_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Facture"
        verbose_name_plural = "Factures"
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.invoice_number

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.invoice_number:
            self.invoice_number = self._generate_invoice_number()
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    @classmethod
    def _generate_invoice_number(cls):
        year = timezone.now().year
        prefix = f"FAC-{year}-"
        last = (
            cls.objects.filter(invoice_number__startswith=prefix)
            .aggregate(max_number=Max("invoice_number"))
            .get("max_number")
        )
        next_seq = int(last.rsplit("-", 1)[-1]) + 1 if last else 1
        return f"{prefix}{next_seq:04d}"

    def recalculate_total(self, commit=True):
        total = self.items.aggregate(total=models.Sum("price"))["total"] or 0
        self.total_amount = total
        if commit:
            self.save(update_fields=["total_amount"])
        return total


class InvoiceItem(models.Model):
    invoice = models.ForeignKey(Invoice, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="items")
    act = models.ForeignKey(MedicalAct, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="invoice_items")
    price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Ligne de facture"
        verbose_name_plural = "Lignes de facture"

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.invoice.invoice_number} â€” {self.act.name}"


========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\apps\billing\services.py ==========

from apps.common.pdf import render_pdf, save_pdf_copy


def generate_receipt_pdf(invoice, save_copy=True):
    patient = invoice.patient
    pdf_bytes = render_pdf(
        "pdf/receipt.html",
        {
            "patient": patient,
            "doc_date": invoice.paid_at or invoice.created_at,
            "invoice": invoice,
            "items": invoice.items.select_related("act"),
        },
    )
    if save_copy:
        save_pdf_copy(patient, "recu", pdf_bytes, reference=invoice.invoice_number)
    return pdf_bytes

from django.urls import path

from . import views

app_name = "billing"

urlpatterns = [
    path("", views.index, name="index"),
    path("<int:pk>/pay/", views.pay_invoice, name="pay_invoice"),
    path("<int:pk>/receipt/", views.download_receipt_pdf, name="invoice_receipt"),
]


========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\apps\billing\views.py ==========

import json

from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.http import Http404, HttpResponse
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, render
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone

from .models import Invoice
from .services import generate_receipt_pdf


@login_required
def index(request):
    invoices = Invoice.objects.select_related("patient").filter(
        cabinet=request.user.profile.cabinet
    )[:50]
    return render(request, "billing/index.html", {"invoices": invoices})


@login_required
def pay_invoice(request, pk):
    cabinet = request.user.profile.cabinet
    invoice = get_object_or_404(Invoice, pk=pk, cabinet=cabinet)
    toast_message = None
    receipt_url = None
    if request.method == "POST":
        payment_method = request.POST.get("payment_method", Invoice.PaymentMethod.CASH)
        invoice.payment_method = payment_method
        invoice.status = Invoice.Status.PAID
        invoice.paid_at = timezone.now()
        invoice.save(update_fields=["payment_method", "status", "paid_at"])
        toast_message = (
            f"Facture {invoice.invoice_number} encaissÃ©e ({invoice.get_payment_method_display()})."
        )
        receipt_url = reverse("billing:invoice_receipt", args=[invoice.pk])

    pending_invoices = Invoice.objects.select_related("patient").filter(
        status=Invoice.Status.PENDING, cabinet=cabinet
    )
    response = render(
        request, "assistant/_pending_invoices.html", {"pending_invoices": pending_invoices}
    )
    if toast_message:
        events = {"toast": {"message": toast_message, "type": "success"}}
        if receipt_url:
            events["receipt-ready"] = {"url": receipt_url}
        response.headers["HX-Trigger"] = json.dumps(events)
    return response


@login_required
def download_receipt_pdf(request, pk):
    invoice = get_object_or_404(Invoice, pk=pk, cabinet=request.user.profile.cabinet)
    if invoice.status != Invoice.Status.PAID:
        raise Http404("ReÃ§u disponible uniquement aprÃ¨s encaissement.")

    pdf_bytes = generate_receipt_pdf(invoice)
    response = HttpResponse(pdf_bytes, content_type="application/pdf")
    response["Content-Disposition"] = f'inline; filename="recu_{invoice.invoice_number}.pdf"'
    return response

from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models
from django.db.models import Sum

from apps.accounts.models import Cabinet
from apps.appointments.models import Appointment
from apps.billing.models import Invoice, InvoiceItem, MedicalAct
from apps.patients.models import Patient


class Consultation(models.Model):
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        DRAFT = "draft", "Brouillon"
        VALIDATED = "validated", "ValidÃ©e"

    cabinet = models.ForeignKey(
        Cabinet, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="consultations", null=True, blank=True
    )
    appointment = models.OneToOneField(
        Appointment, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="consultation"
    )
    patient = models.ForeignKey(
        Patient, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="consultations"
    )
    doctor = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="consultations"
    )
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.DRAFT)
    blood_pressure = models.CharField("Tension", max_length=20, blank=True)
    weight_kg = models.DecimalField(
        "Poids (kg)", max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True
    )
    height_cm = models.DecimalField(
        "Taille (cm)", max_digits=5, decimal_places=1, null=True, blank=True
    )
    clinical_notes = models.TextField(blank=True)
    diagnostic = models.TextField(blank=True)
    last_menstrual_period = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    gestational_age_weeks = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
    ultrasound_notes = models.TextField(blank=True)
    acts = models.ManyToManyField(
        MedicalAct, through="ConsultationAct", related_name="consultations"
    )
    invoice = models.OneToOneField(
        Invoice, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name="consultation"
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Consultation"
        verbose_name_plural = "Consultations"
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Consultation {self.patient} â€” {self.created_at:%d/%m/%Y}"

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        old_status = None
        if self.pk:
            old_status = (
                Consultation.objects.filter(pk=self.pk)
                .values_list("status", flat=True)
                .first()
            )
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)
        if old_status != self.Status.VALIDATED and self.status == self.Status.VALIDATED:
            self._bill()

    def _bill(self):
        """Sum ConsultationAct prices into the linked Invoice (pending), creating it if needed."""
        if self.invoice_id:
            invoice = self.invoice
        else:
            invoice = Invoice.objects.create(
                cabinet=self.cabinet, patient=self.patient, status=Invoice.Status.PENDING
            )
            Consultation.objects.filter(pk=self.pk).update(invoice=invoice)
            self.invoice = invoice

        invoice.items.all().delete()
        for consultation_act in self.consultation_acts.select_related("act"):
            InvoiceItem.objects.create(
                invoice=invoice, act=consultation_act.act, price=consultation_act.price
            )
        total = invoice.items.aggregate(total=Sum("price"))["total"] or 0
        invoice.total_amount = total
        invoice.status = Invoice.Status.PENDING
        invoice.save(update_fields=["total_amount", "status"])


class ConsultationAct(models.Model):
    consultation = models.ForeignKey(
        Consultation, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="consultation_acts"
    )
    act = models.ForeignKey(MedicalAct, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="consultation_acts")
    price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Acte de consultation"
        verbose_name_plural = "Actes de consultation"

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.consultation} â€” {self.act.name}"


class Document(models.Model):
    class DocumentType(models.TextChoices):
        PRESCRIPTION = "prescription", "Ordonnance"
        REPORT = "report", "Compte-rendu"
        LETTER = "letter", "Lettre Ã  un confrÃ¨re"
        CERTIFICATE = "certificate", "Certificat de prise en charge"
        PREANESTHESIA = "preanesthesia", "Consultation prÃ©-anesthÃ©sie"

    consultation = models.ForeignKey(
        Consultation, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="documents"
    )
    document_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=DocumentType.choices)
    content = models.TextField(blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Document"
        verbose_name_plural = "Documents"
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.get_document_type_display()} â€” {self.consultation}"


class PatientDocument(models.Model):
    class DocumentType(models.TextChoices):
        RADIO = "radio", "Radio"
        BILAN = "bilan", "Bilan"
        AUTRE = "autre", "Autre"

    cabinet = models.ForeignKey(
        Cabinet, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="patient_documents", null=True, blank=True
    )
    patient = models.ForeignKey(
        Patient, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="documents_joints"
    )
    title = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    document_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=DocumentType.choices, default=DocumentType.AUTRE)
    file = models.FileField(upload_to="patient_documents/")
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Document patiente"
        verbose_name_plural = "Documents patiente"
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.title} â€” {self.patient}"

def prescription_lines(content):
    return [line.strip() for line in (content or "").splitlines() if line.strip()]


def generate_prescription_pdf(consultation, content, save_copy=True):
    patient = consultation.patient
    pdf_bytes = render_pdf(
        "pdf/prescription.html",
        {
            "patient": patient,
            "doc_date": consultation.updated_at,
            "prescription_lines": prescription_lines(content),
        },
    )
    if save_copy:
        save_pdf_copy(patient, "ordonnance", pdf_bytes, reference=consultation.pk)
    return pdf_bytes


def generate_letter_pdf(consultation, document_type, content, save_copy=True):
    patient = consultation.patient
    doc_title = LETTER_TEMPLATES.get(document_type, {}).get("label", "Document")
    pdf_bytes = render_pdf(
        "pdf/letter.html",
        {
            "patient": patient,
            "doc_date": consultation.updated_at,
            "doc_title": doc_title,
            "content": content,
        },
    )
    if save_copy:
        save_pdf_copy(patient, document_type, pdf_bytes, reference=consultation.pk)
    return pdf_bytes


def generate_report_pdf(consultation, save_copy=True):
    patient = consultation.patient
    gestational_age = None
    if consultation.gestational_age_weeks is not None:
        gestational_age = f"{consultation.gestational_age_weeks} SA"

    pdf_bytes = render_pdf(
        "pdf/consultation_report.html",
        {
            "patient": patient,
            "doc_date": consultation.updated_at,
            "reason": consultation.appointment.reason,
            "last_menstrual_period": consultation.last_menstrual_period,
            "gestational_age": gestational_age,
            "ultrasound_notes": consultation.ultrasound_notes,
            "diagnostic": consultation.diagnostic,
            "clinical_notes": consultation.clinical_notes,
        },
    )
    if save_copy:
        save_pdf_copy(patient, "compte_rendu", pdf_bytes, reference=consultation.pk)
    return pdf_bytes


def generate_patient_record_pdf(patient, save_copy=True):
    consultations = (
        patient.consultations.select_related("appointment")
        .order_by("-created_at")[:20]
    )
    pdf_bytes = render_pdf(
        "pdf/patient_record.html",
        {
            "patient": patient,
            "doc_date": timezone.now(),
            "consultations": consultations,
        },
    )
    if save_copy:
        save_pdf_copy(patient, "dossier_patient", pdf_bytes)
    return pdf_bytes

import io
import zipfile
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.http import HttpResponse
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render
from django.utils import timezone
from django.utils.text import slugify

from apps.accounts.decorators import role_required
from apps.appointments.models import Appointment
from apps.billing.models import MedicalAct
from apps.patients.models import Patient

from .models import Consultation, ConsultationAct, Document, PatientDocument
from .services import (
    LETTER_TEMPLATES,
    PRESCRIPTION_TEMPLATES,
    generate_letter_pdf,
    generate_patient_record_pdf,
    generate_prescription_pdf,
    generate_report_pdf,
)

LETTER_DOCUMENT_TYPES = {
    "letter": Document.DocumentType.LETTER,
    "certificate": Document.DocumentType.CERTIFICATE,
    "preanesthesia": Document.DocumentType.PREANESTHESIA,
}

ACTIVE_QUEUE_STATUSES = [
    Appointment.Status.ARRIVED,
    Appointment.Status.WAITING,
    Appointment.Status.IN_CONSULTATION,
]


def _parse_date(value):
    if not value:
        return None
    try:
        return datetime.strptime(value, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
    except ValueError:
        return None


@login_required
@role_required("doctor")
def doctor_dashboard(request):
    cabinet = request.user.profile.cabinet
    today = timezone.localdate()
    selected_date = _parse_date(request.GET.get("date")) or today
    is_today = selected_date == today

    day_appointments = (
        Appointment.objects.filter(cabinet=cabinet, date__date=selected_date)
        .exclude(status=Appointment.Status.CANCELLED)
        .select_related("patient")
        .order_by("date")
    )

    if is_today:
        queue = day_appointments.filter(status__in=ACTIVE_QUEUE_STATUSES)
    else:
        # Statuts de file (arrivÃ©e/attente/en consultation) n'ont de sens qu'au jour J :
        # pour une autre date, on affiche tous les rendez-vous programmÃ©s de ce jour.
        queue = day_appointments

    next_patient = queue.first() if is_today else None

    return render(
        request,
        "doctor/dashboard.html",
        {
            "queue": queue,
            "next_patient": next_patient,
            "now": timezone.now(),
            "selected_date": selected_date,
            "is_today": is_today,
            "prev_date": selected_date - timedelta(days=1),
            "next_date": selected_date + timedelta(days=1),
            "today": today,
            "today_total": day_appointments.count(),
            "in_consultation_count": day_appointments.filter(
                status=Appointment.Status.IN_CONSULTATION
            ).count(),
            "completed_today": day_appointments.filter(
                status=Appointment.Status.COMPLETED
            ).count(),
        },
    )


@login_required
@role_required("doctor")
def consultation_detail(request, appointment_id):
    cabinet = request.user.profile.cabinet
    appointment = get_object_or_404(
        Appointment.objects.select_related("patient"),
        pk=appointment_id,
        cabinet=cabinet,
    )
    consultation, _ = Consultation.objects.get_or_create(
        appointment=appointment,
        defaults={"patient": appointment.patient, "doctor": request.user, "cabinet": cabinet},
    )

    if request.method == "POST":
        action = request.POST.get("action")

        consultation.blood_pressure = request.POST.get("blood_pressure", "")
        weight = request.POST.get("weight_kg") or None
        consultation.weight_kg = weight
        height = request.POST.get("height_cm") or None
        consultation.height_cm = height
        consultation.clinical_notes = request.POST.get("clinical_notes", "")
        consultation.diagnostic = request.POST.get("diagnostic", "")
        consultation.last_menstrual_period = request.POST.get("last_menstrual_period") or None
        ga_weeks = request.POST.get("gestational_age_weeks") or None
        consultation.gestational_age_weeks = int(ga_weeks) if ga_weeks else None
        consultation.ultrasound_notes = request.POST.get("ultrasound_notes", "")
        if not consultation.doctor_id:
            consultation.doctor = request.user

        act_ids = [int(v) for v in request.POST.getlist("acts") if v]
        selected_acts = MedicalAct.objects.filter(id__in=act_ids)

        consultation.consultation_acts.exclude(act_id__in=act_ids).delete()
        existing_act_ids = set(consultation.consultation_acts.values_list("act_id", flat=True))
        for act in selected_acts:
            if act.id not in existing_act_ids:
                ConsultationAct.objects.create(consultation=consultation, act=act, price=act.price)

        prescription_content = request.POST.get("prescription_content", "").strip()
        if prescription_content:
            Document.objects.update_or_create(
                consultation=consultation,
                document_type=Document.DocumentType.PRESCRIPTION,
                defaults={"content": prescription_content},
            )

        letter_type = request.POST.get("letter_type", "")
        letter_content = request.POST.get("letter_content", "").strip()
        if letter_content and letter_type in LETTER_DOCUMENT_TYPES:
            Document.objects.update_or_create(
                consultation=consultation,
                document_type=LETTER_DOCUMENT_TYPES[letter_type],
                defaults={"content": letter_content},
            )

        consultation.status = (
            Consultation.Status.VALIDATED if action == "validate" else Consultation.Status.DRAFT
        )
        consultation.save()

        if action == "validate":
            appointment.status = Appointment.Status.COMPLETED
            appointment.save(update_fields=["status"])
            messages.success(
                request,
                f"Consultation validÃ©e et facture {consultation.invoice.invoice_number} transmise.",
            )
            return redirect("clinical:doctor_dashboard")

        messages.success(request, "Brouillon enregistrÃ©.")
        return redirect("clinical:consultation_detail", appointment_id=appointment.pk)

    all_acts = MedicalAct.objects.filter(is_active=True)
    selected_act_ids = list(
        consultation.consultation_acts.values_list("act_id", flat=True)
    )
    acts_json = [
        {
            "id": act.id,
            "code": act.code,
            "name": act.name,
            "price": float(act.price),
            "is_quick_exam": act.is_quick_exam,
        }
        for act in all_acts
    ]
    prescription_doc = consultation.documents.filter(
        document_type=Document.DocumentType.PRESCRIPTION
    ).first()
    prescription_templates_json = [
        {"key": key, "label": tpl["label"], "text": tpl["text"]}
        for key, tpl in PRESCRIPTION_TEMPLATES.items()
    ]
    letter_templates_json = [
        {"key": key, "label": tpl["label"], "text": tpl["text"]}
        for key, tpl in LETTER_TEMPLATES.items()
    ]
    letters_by_type = {
        doc.document_type: doc.content
        for doc in consultation.documents.filter(
            document_type__in=LETTER_DOCUMENT_TYPES.values()
        )
    }
    letters_json = {
        key: letters_by_type.get(doc_type, "")
        for key, doc_type in LETTER_DOCUMENT_TYPES.items()
    }
    patient_documents = PatientDocument.objects.filter(
        cabinet=cabinet, patient=appointment.patient
    )
    return render(
        request,
        "doctor/consultation.html",
        {
            "appointment": appointment,
            "patient": appointment.patient,
            "consultation": consultation,
            "all_acts": all_acts,
            "selected_act_ids": selected_act_ids,
            "acts_json": acts_json,
            "prescription_content": prescription_doc.content if prescription_doc else "",
            "prescription_templates_json": prescription_templates_json,
            "letter_templates_json": letter_templates_json,
            "letters_json": letters_json,
            "patient_documents": patient_documents,
            "document_type_choices": PatientDocument.DocumentType.choices,
        },
    )


@login_required
@role_required("doctor")
def upload_patient_document(request, appointment_id):
    cabinet = request.user.profile.cabinet
    appointment = get_object_or_404(Appointment, pk=appointment_id, cabinet=cabinet)
    if request.method == "POST" and request.FILES.get("file"):
        title = request.POST.get("title", "").strip() or request.FILES["file"].name
        document_type = request.POST.get("document_type", PatientDocument.DocumentType.AUTRE)
        if document_type not in PatientDocument.DocumentType.values:
            document_type = PatientDocument.DocumentType.AUTRE
        PatientDocument.objects.create(
            cabinet=cabinet,
            patient=appointment.patient,
            title=title,
            document_type=document_type,
            file=request.FILES["file"],
        )
        messages.success(request, "Document ajoutÃ© au dossier de la patiente.")
    else:
        messages.error(request, "Veuillez sÃ©lectionner un fichier Ã  tÃ©lÃ©verser.")
    return redirect("clinical:consultation_detail", appointment_id=appointment.pk)

@login_required
def download_full_patient_archive(request, patient_id):
    """ZIP contenant le PDF rÃ©capitulatif du dossier + tous les documents joints (radios/bilans)."""
    cabinet = request.user.profile.cabinet
    patient = get_object_or_404(Patient, pk=patient_id, cabinet=cabinet)

    pdf_bytes = generate_patient_record_pdf(patient, save_copy=False)
    documents = PatientDocument.objects.filter(cabinet=cabinet, patient=patient)

    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    with zipfile.ZipFile(buffer, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:
        archive.writestr("patient_record.pdf", pdf_bytes)
        used_names = set()
        for doc in documents:
            if not doc.file:
                continue
            base_name = doc.file.name.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
            arcname = f"documents/{base_name}"
            suffix = 1
            while arcname in used_names:
                stem, _, ext = base_name.rpartition(".")
                arcname = f"documents/{stem or base_name}_{suffix}{'.' + ext if ext else ''}"
                suffix += 1
            used_names.add(arcname)
            doc.file.open("rb")
            try:
                archive.writestr(arcname, doc.file.read())
            finally:
                doc.file.close()

    slug = slugify(f"{patient.first_name}-{patient.last_name}") or patient.patient_number
    response = HttpResponse(buffer.getvalue(), content_type="application/zip")
    response["Content-Disposition"] = f'attachment; filename="Dossier_{slug}.zip"'
    return response

========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\apps\common\pdf.py ==========

import io
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings
from django.template.loader import render_to_string
from django.utils import timezone
from xhtml2pdf import pisa


def render_pdf(template_name, context):
    """Render a Django template to PDF bytes via xhtml2pdf (pure Python, no native deps)."""
    html_string = render_to_string(template_name, context)
    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    result = pisa.CreatePDF(src=html_string, dest=buffer)
    if result.err:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Ã‰chec de la gÃ©nÃ©ration PDF pour {template_name}")
    return buffer.getvalue()


def save_pdf_copy(patient, document_kind, pdf_bytes, reference=""):
    """Archive a generated PDF under media/documents/<patient_number>/<date>/.

    Returns the path written, relative to MEDIA_ROOT.
    """
    today = timezone.localdate().isoformat()
    directory = Path(settings.MEDIA_ROOT) / "documents" / patient.patient_number / today
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    suffix = f"_{reference}" if reference else ""
    timestamp = timezone.now().strftime("%H%M%S")
    filename = f"{document_kind}{suffix}_{timestamp}.pdf"
    file_path = directory / filename
    file_path.write_bytes(pdf_bytes)
    return file_path.relative_to(settings.MEDIA_ROOT)


========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\apps\common\whatsapp.py ==========

from urllib.parse import quote


def clean_moroccan_phone(raw_phone):
    """Nettoie un numÃ©ro pour le format international wa.me (ex: 0612345678 -> 212612345678)."""
    digits = "".join(ch for ch in (raw_phone or "") if ch.isdigit())
    if not digits:
        return ""
    if digits.startswith("00"):
        digits = digits[2:]
    if len(digits) == 10 and digits.startswith("0"):
        digits = "212" + digits[1:]
    elif digits.startswith("0") and len(digits) > 10:
        digits = "212" + digits.lstrip("0")
    return digits


def appointment_reminder_link(patient, date_display, time_display):
    phone = clean_moroccan_phone(getattr(patient, "phone", ""))
    if not phone:
        return ""
    message = (
        f"Bonjour {patient.first_name}, le Cabinet du Dr ERRMILI vous rappelle votre "
        f"rendez-vous prÃ©vu le {date_display} Ã  {time_display}. "
        "Merci de confirmer votre prÃ©sence."
    )
    return f"https://wa.me/{phone}?text={quote(message)}"

from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models

from apps.accounts.models import Cabinet
from apps.patients.models import Patient


class Task(models.Model):
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = "pending", "Ã€ faire"
        IN_PROGRESS = "in_progress", "En cours"
        COMPLETED = "completed", "TerminÃ©e"

    class Priority(models.TextChoices):
        NORMAL = "normal", "Normale"
        URGENT = "urgent", "Urgente"

    cabinet = models.ForeignKey(
        Cabinet, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="tasks", null=True, blank=True
    )
    author = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="authored_tasks"
    )
    assignee = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="assigned_tasks"
    )
    title = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)
    patient = models.ForeignKey(
        Patient, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name="tasks"
    )
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.PENDING)
    priority = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=Priority.choices, default=Priority.NORMAL)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "TÃ¢che"
        verbose_name_plural = "TÃ¢ches"
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.title

import json

from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render

from apps.accounts.decorators import role_required
from apps.accounts.models import Profile
from apps.patients.models import Patient

from .models import Task


@login_required
def index(request):
    return render(request, "communications/index.html", {"tasks": _active_tasks_for(request.user)})


def _active_tasks_for(user):
    return (
        Task.objects.select_related("author", "patient")
        .filter(assignee=user, cabinet=user.profile.cabinet)
        .exclude(status=Task.Status.COMPLETED)
        .order_by("-priority", "-created_at")
    )


@login_required
def tasks_panel(request):
    return render(
        request, "communications/_tasks_panel.html", {"tasks": _active_tasks_for(request.user)}
    )


@login_required
def toggle_task(request, pk):
    task = get_object_or_404(
        Task, pk=pk, assignee=request.user, cabinet=request.user.profile.cabinet
    )
    toast_message = None
    if request.method == "POST":
        task.status = Task.Status.COMPLETED
        task.save(update_fields=["status"])
        toast_message = f"TÃ¢che Â« {task.title} Â» marquÃ©e terminÃ©e."

    response = render(
        request, "communications/_tasks_panel.html", {"tasks": _active_tasks_for(request.user)}
    )
    if toast_message:
        response.headers["HX-Trigger"] = json.dumps(
            {"toast": {"message": toast_message, "type": "success"}}
        )
    return response


@login_required
@role_required("doctor")
def create_task(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        cabinet = request.user.profile.cabinet
        title = request.POST.get("title", "").strip()
        if title:
            assignee = User.objects.filter(
                profile__role=Profile.Role.ASSISTANT, profile__cabinet=cabinet
            ).first()
            patient_id = request.POST.get("patient") or None
            patient = (
                Patient.objects.filter(pk=patient_id, cabinet=cabinet).first()
                if patient_id
                else None
            )
            Task.objects.create(
                cabinet=cabinet,
                author=request.user,
                assignee=assignee or request.user,
                title=title,
                description=request.POST.get("description", ""),
                patient=patient,
                priority=request.POST.get("priority", Task.Priority.NORMAL),
            )
            messages.success(request, "Consigne envoyÃ©e Ã  l'assistante.")
    return redirect("clinical:doctor_dashboard")

========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\apps\patients\models.py ==========

from django.db import models
from django.db.models import Max
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import Cabinet


class Patient(models.Model):
    class BloodGroup(models.TextChoices):
        A_POS = "A+", "A+"
        A_NEG = "A-", "A-"
        B_POS = "B+", "B+"
        B_NEG = "B-", "B-"
        AB_POS = "AB+", "AB+"
        AB_NEG = "AB-", "AB-"
        O_POS = "O+", "O+"
        O_NEG = "O-", "O-"

    cabinet = models.ForeignKey(
        Cabinet, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="patients", null=True, blank=True
    )
    patient_number = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True, editable=False)
    first_name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    last_name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    cin = models.CharField("CIN", max_length=20, blank=True)
    social_security_number = models.CharField(
        "NÂ° de SÃ©curitÃ© Sociale", max_length=20, blank=True
    )
    organisme_social = models.CharField(
        "Organisme de sÃ©curitÃ© sociale", max_length=100, blank=True
    )
    birth_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    phone = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True)
    emergency_contact = models.CharField(max_length=150, blank=True)
    blood_group = models.CharField(
        max_length=3, choices=BloodGroup.choices, blank=True
    )
    allergies = models.TextField(blank=True)
    medical_history = models.TextField(blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Patiente"
        verbose_name_plural = "Patientes"
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.patient_number} â€” {self.first_name} {self.last_name}"

    @property
    def age(self):
        if not self.birth_date:
            return None
        today = timezone.now().date()
        years = today.year - self.birth_date.year
        if (today.month, today.day) < (self.birth_date.month, self.birth_date.day):
            years -= 1
        return years

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.patient_number:
            self.patient_number = self._generate_patient_number()
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    @classmethod
    def _generate_patient_number(cls):
        year = timezone.now().year
        prefix = f"PAT-{year}-"
        last = (
            cls.objects.filter(patient_number__startswith=prefix)
            .aggregate(max_number=Max("patient_number"))
            .get("max_number")
        )
        next_seq = int(last.rsplit("-", 1)[-1]) + 1 if last else 1
        return f"{prefix}{next_seq:04d}"

from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.appointments.models import Appointment
from apps.clinical.models import Consultation, PatientDocument

from .models import Patient


@login_required
def index(request):
    cabinet = request.user.profile.cabinet
    patients = list(Patient.objects.filter(cabinet=cabinet)[:50])
    upcoming = (
        Appointment.objects.select_related("patient")
        .filter(cabinet=cabinet, patient__in=patients, date__gte=timezone.now())
        .exclude(status=Appointment.Status.CANCELLED)
        .order_by("date")
    )
    next_appointment_by_patient = {}
    for appt in upcoming:
        next_appointment_by_patient.setdefault(appt.patient_id, appt)

    completed_consultations = (
        Consultation.objects.select_related("appointment")
        .filter(cabinet=cabinet, patient__in=patients, status=Consultation.Status.VALIDATED)
        .order_by("-created_at")
    )
    last_completed_by_patient = {}
    for consultation in completed_consultations:
        last_completed_by_patient.setdefault(consultation.patient_id, consultation)

    for patient in patients:
        patient.next_appointment = next_appointment_by_patient.get(patient.pk)
        patient.last_completed_consultation = last_completed_by_patient.get(patient.pk)

    view_filter = request.GET.get("view", "all")
    if view_filter == "completed":
        patients = [p for p in patients if p.last_completed_consultation]

    return render(
        request,
        "patients/index.html",
        {
            "patients": patients,
            "view_filter": view_filter,
            "completed_count": len(last_completed_by_patient),
        },
    )


@login_required
def create_patient(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        first_name = request.POST.get("first_name", "").strip()
        last_name = request.POST.get("last_name", "").strip()
        if first_name and last_name:
            patient = Patient.objects.create(
                cabinet=request.user.profile.cabinet,
                first_name=first_name,
                last_name=last_name,
                cin=request.POST.get("cin", "").strip(),
                social_security_number=request.POST.get("social_security_number", "").strip(),
                organisme_social=request.POST.get("organisme_social", "").strip(),
                birth_date=request.POST.get("birth_date") or None,
                phone=request.POST.get("phone", "").strip(),
                emergency_contact=request.POST.get("emergency_contact", "").strip(),
                blood_group=request.POST.get("blood_group", "").strip(),
            )
            messages.success(request, f"Patiente {patient} crÃ©Ã©e ({patient.patient_number}).")
            return redirect("appointments:create_appointment_for", patient_id=patient.pk)
        messages.error(request, "Nom et prÃ©nom sont obligatoires.")

    return render(request, "patients/create.html")


@login_required
def edit_patient(request, pk):
    patient = get_object_or_404(Patient, pk=pk, cabinet=request.user.profile.cabinet)
    if request.method == "POST":
        first_name = request.POST.get("first_name", "").strip()
        last_name = request.POST.get("last_name", "").strip()
        if first_name and last_name:
            patient.first_name = first_name
            patient.last_name = last_name
            patient.cin = request.POST.get("cin", "").strip()
            patient.social_security_number = request.POST.get("social_security_number", "").strip()
            patient.organisme_social = request.POST.get("organisme_social", "").strip()
            patient.birth_date = request.POST.get("birth_date") or None
            patient.phone = request.POST.get("phone", "").strip()
            patient.emergency_contact = request.POST.get("emergency_contact", "").strip()
            patient.blood_group = request.POST.get("blood_group", "").strip()
            patient.save()
            messages.success(request, f"Dossier de {patient} mis Ã  jour.")
            return redirect("patients:index")
        messages.error(request, "Nom et prÃ©nom sont obligatoires.")

    return render(request, "patients/edit.html", {"patient": patient})


@login_required
def patient_documents(request, pk):
    patient = get_object_or_404(Patient, pk=pk, cabinet=request.user.profile.cabinet)
    if request.method == "POST" and request.FILES.get("file"):
        title = request.POST.get("title", "").strip() or request.FILES["file"].name
        document_type = request.POST.get("document_type", PatientDocument.DocumentType.AUTRE)
        if document_type not in PatientDocument.DocumentType.values:
            document_type = PatientDocument.DocumentType.AUTRE
        PatientDocument.objects.create(
            cabinet=request.user.profile.cabinet,
            patient=patient,
            title=title,
            document_type=document_type,
            file=request.FILES["file"],
        )
        messages.success(request, "Document ajoutÃ© au dossier de la patiente.")
        return redirect("patients:patient_documents", pk=patient.pk)

    documents = PatientDocument.objects.filter(cabinet=request.user.profile.cabinet, patient=patient)
    return render(
        request,
        "patients/documents.html",
        {
            "patient": patient,
            "documents": documents,
            "document_type_choices": PatientDocument.DocumentType.choices,
        },
    )

from apps.accounts.views import tenant_login

    path("admin/", admin.site.urls),
    path("accounts/", include("apps.accounts.urls")),

    path("appointments/", include("apps.appointments.urls")),
    path("clinical/", include("apps.clinical.urls")),

]
