<script>
    function consultationForm() {
        const letters = JSON.parse(document.getElementById("letters-data").textContent);
        return {
            action: "draft",
            lmp: "{{ consultation.last_menstrual_period|date:'Y-m-d' }}",
            acts: JSON.parse(document.getElementById("acts-data").textContent),
            selected: JSON.parse(document.getElementById("selected-acts-data").textContent),
            prescriptionTemplates: JSON.parse(document.getElementById("prescription-templates-data").textContent),
            prescriptionContent: JSON.parse(document.getElementById("prescription-content-data").textContent),
            letterTemplates: JSON.parse(document.getElementById("letter-templates-data").textContent),
            letters: letters,
            letterType: "letter",
            letterContent: letters.letter || "",
            insertTemplate(tpl) {
                this.prescriptionContent = this.prescriptionContent
                    ? this.prescriptionContent + "\n" + tpl.text
                    : tpl.text;
            },
            insertLetterTemplate(tpl) {
                this.letterType = tpl.key;
                this.letterContent = this.letterContent
                    ? this.letterContent + "\n" + tpl.text
                    : tpl.text;
            },
            onLetterTypeChange() {
                this.letterContent = this.letters[this.letterType] || "";
            },
            toggle(id) {
                if (this.selected.includes(id)) {
                    this.selected = this.selected.filter((x) => x !== id);
                } else {
                    this.selected.push(id);
                }
            },
            get quickExamActs() {
                return this.acts.filter((a) => a.is_quick_exam);
            },
            get letterTypeLabel() {
                const tpl = this.letterTemplates.find((t) => t.key === this.letterType);
                return tpl ? tpl.label : "Document";
            },
            get letterPdfUrl() {
                return "{% url 'clinical:letter_pdf' appointment.pk 'TYPE_PLACEHOLDER' %}".replace(
                    "TYPE_PLACEHOLDER",
                    this.letterType
                );
            },
            get total() {
                return this.acts
                    .filter((a) => this.selected.includes(a.id))
                    .reduce((sum, a) => sum + a.price, 0);
            },
            get ga() {
                if (!this.lmp) return null;
                const diffMs = new Date() - new Date(this.lmp + "T00:00:00");
                const diffDays = Math.floor(diffMs / 86400000);
                if (diffDays < 0) return null;
                return { weeks: Math.floor(diffDays / 7), days: diffDays % 7 };
            },
            beforeSubmit() {
                if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
            },
        };
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

            <p class="text-sm leading-snug" x-text="toast.message"></p>
        </div>
    </template>
</div>

<script>
    function toastHub() {
        return {
            toasts: [],
            push(detail) {
                const id = Date.now() + Math.random();
                this.toasts.push({ id, message: detail.message || detail, type: detail.type || "success" });
                if (this.toasts.length > 3) this.toasts.splice(0, this.toasts.length - 3);
                this.$nextTick(() => lucide.createIcons());
                setTimeout(() => {
                    this.toasts = this.toasts.filter((t) => t.id !== id);
                }, 3500);
            },
        };
    }

    document.body.addEventListener("htmx:afterSettle", () => {
        if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
    });

    // HTMX responses can set the HX-Trigger header to:  {"toast": {"message": "...", "type": "success"}}
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
        if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
    });

    // Barre de progression globale (feedback "en cours" style Linear/Vercel) sur toute requÃªte HTMX
    (function () {
        const bar = document.getElementById("htmx-progress-bar");
        let hideTimeout;
        document.body.addEventListener("htmx:beforeRequest", () => {
            clearTimeout(hideTimeout);
            bar.classList.add("is-active");
            bar.style.transform = "scaleX(0.3)";
        });
        document.body.addEventListener("htmx:afterRequest", () => {
            bar.style.transform = "scaleX(1)";
            hideTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
                bar.classList.remove("is-active");
                bar.style.transform = "scaleX(0)";
            }, 250);
        });
    })();
</script>
</body>
</html>

========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\tests\test_full_flow.py ==========

from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.test import Client, TestCase, override_settings
from django.urls import reverse

from apps.accounts.models import Profile
from apps.appointments.models import Appointment
from apps.billing.models import Invoice, MedicalAct
from apps.clinical.models import Consultation
from apps.communications.models import Task
from apps.patients.models import Patient


@override_settings(ALLOWED_HOSTS=["testserver"])
class FullClinicJourneyTest(TestCase):
    """End-to-end regression test: Connexion -> Patiente -> RDV -> Consultation
    -> Actes -> Ordonnance PDF -> Encaissement -> ReÃ§u PDF -> TÃ¢ches."""

    def setUp(self):
        self.doctor = User.objects.create_user(username="dr_test", password="testpass123")
        Profile.objects.update_or_create(user=self.doctor, defaults={"role": Profile.Role.DOCTOR})

        self.assistant = User.objects.create_user(username="assist_test", password="testpass123")
        Profile.objects.update_or_create(
            user=self.assistant, defaults={"role": Profile.Role.ASSISTANT}
        )

        self.act_consultation = MedicalAct.objects.create(
            code="CONS_TEST", name="Consultation test", price=300
        )
        self.act_echo = MedicalAct.objects.create(code="ECHO_TEST", name="Ã‰cho test", price=500)

        self.assistant_client = Client()
        self.doctor_client = Client()

    def test_full_patient_journey(self):
        # 1. Connexion â€” l'assistante se connecte et est redirigÃ©e vers son tableau de bord
        self.assertTrue(
            self.assistant_client.login(username="assist_test", password="testpass123")
        )
        response = self.assistant_client.get(reverse("accounts:post_login_redirect"), follow=True)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(response.redirect_chain[-1][0], reverse("appointments:assistant_dashboard"))

        self.assertTrue(self.doctor_client.login(username="dr_test", password="testpass123"))
        response = self.doctor_client.get(reverse("accounts:post_login_redirect"), follow=True)
        self.assertEqual(response.redirect_chain[-1][0], reverse("clinical:doctor_dashboard"))

        # Un mÃ©decin ne peut jamais accÃ©der au tableau de bord assistante ? (autorisÃ©,
        # seule la zone clinique est verrouillÃ©e) â€” vÃ©rifiÃ© sÃ©parÃ©ment plus bas.

        # 2. CrÃ©ation patiente par l'assistante
        response = self.assistant_client.post(
            reverse("patients:create_patient"),
            {
                "first_name": "Fatima",
                "last_name": "Testaoui",
                "cin": "TT123456",
                "phone": "0600000000",
                "allergies": "PÃ©nicilline",
                "medical_history": "RAS",
            },
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        patient = Patient.objects.get(cin="TT123456")
        self.assertTrue(patient.patient_number.startswith("PAT-"))

        # 3. Prise de RDV pour cette patiente
        response = self.assistant_client.post(
            reverse("appointments:create_appointment_for", args=[patient.pk]),
            {"date": "2026-09-01T10:00", "duration_minutes": "30", "reason": "Consultation de suivi"},
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        appointment = Appointment.objects.get(patient=patient)
        self.assertEqual(appointment.status, Appointment.Status.SCHEDULED)

        # Assistante fait progresser la file jusqu'Ã  "en consultation"
        for _ in range(3):
            response = self.assistant_client.post(
                reverse("appointments:advance_status", args=[appointment.pk])
            )
            self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        appointment.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(appointment.status, Appointment.Status.IN_CONSULTATION)

        # Une assistante ne peut jamais accÃ©der aux URLs cliniques
        response = self.assistant_client.get(reverse("clinical:doctor_dashboard"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 403)

        # 4. Consultation mÃ©decin : accÃ¨s Ã  la fiche
        response = self.doctor_client.get(
            reverse("clinical:consultation_detail", args=[appointment.pk])
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        consultation = Consultation.objects.get(appointment=appointment)

        # 5. Actes rÃ©alisÃ©s + validation -> facture auto-gÃ©nÃ©rÃ©e
        response = self.doctor_client.post(
            reverse("clinical:consultation_detail", args=[appointment.pk]),
            {
                "action": "validate",
                "blood_pressure": "12/8",
                "weight_kg": "62",
                "clinical_notes": "Examen sans particularitÃ©.",
                "diagnostic": "Suivi normal.",
                "last_menstrual_period": "2026-06-01",
                "gestational_age_weeks": "12",
                "ultrasound_notes": "BIP 45mm, LF 30mm",
                "prescription_content": "Acide folique 0,4 mg â€” 1 comprimÃ© par jour",
                "acts": [str(self.act_consultation.pk), str(self.act_echo.pk)],
            },
            follow=True,
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        consultation.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(consultation.status, Consultation.Status.VALIDATED)
        self.assertIsNotNone(consultation.invoice)
        invoice = consultation.invoice
        self.assertEqual(invoice.total_amount, 800)
        self.assertEqual(invoice.status, Invoice.Status.PENDING)

        appointment.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(appointment.status, Appointment.Status.COMPLETED)

        # 6. TÃ©lÃ©chargement de l'ordonnance PDF
        response = self.doctor_client.get(
            reverse("clinical:prescription_pdf", args=[appointment.pk])
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(response["Content-Type"], "application/pdf")
        self.assertTrue(response.content.startswith(b"%PDF"))

        # Une assistante ne peut pas non plus tÃ©lÃ©charger un document clinique
        response = self.assistant_client.get(
            reverse("clinical:prescription_pdf", args=[appointment.pk])
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 403)

        # 7. Encaissement par l'assistante
        response = self.assistant_client.post(
            reverse("billing:pay_invoice", args=[invoice.pk]), {"payment_method": "cash"}
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        invoice.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(invoice.status, Invoice.Status.PAID)
        self.assertEqual(invoice.payment_method, "cash")
        self.assertIsNotNone(invoice.paid_at)

        # 8. TÃ©lÃ©chargement du reÃ§u PDF
        response = self.assistant_client.get(reverse("billing:invoice_receipt", args=[invoice.pk]))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(response["Content-Type"], "application/pdf")
        self.assertTrue(response.content.startswith(b"%PDF"))

        # 9. TÃ¢ches â€” le mÃ©decin envoie une consigne, l'assistante la termine
        response = self.doctor_client.post(
            reverse("communications:create_task"),
            {
                "title": "Rappeler la patiente pour rÃ©sultat frottis",
                "description": "RÃ©sultats attendus sous 48h",
                "patient": str(patient.pk),
                "priority": "urgent",
            },
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        task = Task.objects.get(patient=patient)
        self.assertEqual(task.assignee, self.assistant)
        self.assertEqual(task.status, Task.Status.PENDING)

        response = self.assistant_client.get(reverse("communications:tasks_panel"))
        self.assertContains(response, "Rappeler la patiente")

        response = self.assistant_client.post(reverse("communications:toggle_task", args=[task.pk]))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        task.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(task.status, Task.Status.COMPLETED)

        response = self.assistant_client.get(reverse("communications:tasks_panel"))
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Rappeler la patiente")


========== FICHIER : C:\Users\TOUFIK\Desktop\Medigest\tests\test_intake_vitals.py ==========

from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.test import Client, TestCase, override_settings
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import Cabinet, Profile
from apps.appointments.models import Appointment
from apps.clinical.models import Consultation
from apps.patients.models import Patient


@override_settings(ALLOWED_HOSTS=["testserver"])
class IntakeVitalsTest(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.cabinet = Cabinet.objects.create(nom="Cabinet Test", slug="cabinet-test")

        self.doctor = User.objects.create_user(username="dr_vitals", password="testpass123")
        Profile.objects.update_or_create(
            user=self.doctor, defaults={"role": Profile.Role.DOCTOR, "cabinet": self.cabinet}
        )

        self.assistant = User.objects.create_user(username="assist_vitals", password="testpass123")
        Profile.objects.update_or_create(
            user=self.assistant, defaults={"role": Profile.Role.ASSISTANT, "cabinet": self.cabinet}
        )

        self.patient = Patient.objects.create(
            cabinet=self.cabinet, first_name="Salma", last_name="Vitals"
        )
        self.appointment = Appointment.objects.create(
            cabinet=self.cabinet,
            patient=self.patient,
            date=timezone.now(),
            status=Appointment.Status.WAITING,
        )

        self.client = Client()
        self.client.login(username="assist_vitals", password="testpass123")

    def test_assistant_creates_consultation_and_saves_vitals(self):
        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("appointments:save_intake_vitals", args=[self.appointment.pk]),
            {
                "blood_pressure": "12/8",
                "weight_kg": "68.5",
                "height_cm": "165",
                "last_menstrual_period": "2026-08-01",
            },
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)

        consultation = Consultation.objects.get(appointment=self.appointment)
        self.assertEqual(consultation.doctor, self.doctor)
        self.assertEqual(consultation.blood_pressure, "12/8")
        self.assertEqual(str(consultation.weight_kg), "68.50")
        self.assertEqual(str(consultation.height_cm), "165.0")
        self.assertEqual(str(consultation.last_menstrual_period), "2026-08-01")

    def test_assistant_cannot_set_clinical_fields(self):
        self.client.post(
            reverse("appointments:save_intake_vitals", args=[self.appointment.pk]),
            {
                "blood_pressure": "12/8",
                "diagnostic": "Tentative d'injection de diagnostic",
                "clinical_notes": "Tentative d'injection de notes",
                "ultrasound_notes": "Tentative d'injection d'Ã©chographie",
            },
        )
        consultation = Consultation.objects.get(appointment=self.appointment)
        self.assertEqual(consultation.diagnostic, "")
        self.assertEqual(consultation.clinical_notes, "")
        self.assertEqual(consultation.ultrasound_notes, "")

    def test_second_save_updates_existing_consultation_without_duplicate(self):
        url = reverse("appointments:save_intake_vitals", args=[self.appointment.pk])
        self.client.post(url, {"blood_pressure": "12/8", "weight_kg": "68"})
        self.client.post(url, {"blood_pressure": "13/9", "weight_kg": "69"})

        self.assertEqual(Consultation.objects.filter(appointment=self.appointment).count(), 1)
        consultation = Consultation.objects.get(appointment=self.appointment)
        self.assertEqual(consultation.blood_pressure, "13/9")
        self.assertEqual(str(consultation.weight_kg), "69.00")

    def test_doctor_sees_prefilled_vitals(self):
        self.client.post(
            reverse("appointments:save_intake_vitals", args=[self.appointment.pk]),
            {"blood_pressure": "12/8", "weight_kg": "68", "height_cm": "165"},
        )
        doctor_client = Client()
        doctor_client.login(username="dr_vitals", password="testpass123")
        response = doctor_client.get(
            reverse("clinical:consultation_detail", args=[self.appointment.pk])
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "12/8")

    def test_no_doctor_in_cabinet_does_not_crash(self):
        empty_cabinet = Cabinet.objects.create(nom="Cabinet Vide", slug="cabinet-vide")
        assistant_no_doctor = User.objects.create_user(
            username="assist_alone", password="testpass123"
        )
        Profile.objects.update_or_create(
            user=assistant_no_doctor,
            defaults={"role": Profile.Role.ASSISTANT, "cabinet": empty_cabinet},
        )
        patient = Patient.objects.create(
            cabinet=empty_cabinet, first_name="Nadia", last_name="Solo"
        )
        appointment = Appointment.objects.create(
            cabinet=empty_cabinet,
            patient=patient,
            date=timezone.now(),
            status=Appointment.Status.ARRIVED,
        )
        client = Client()
        client.login(username="assist_alone", password="testpass123")

        response = client.post(
            reverse("appointments:save_intake_vitals", args=[appointment.pk]),
            {"blood_pressure": "12/8"},
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertFalse(Consultation.objects.filter(appointment=appointment).exists())

#!/usr/bin/env python
import os
import sys


def main():
    os.environ.setdefault("DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE", "config.settings")
    try:
        from django.core.management import execute_from_command_line
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise ImportError(
            "Couldn't import Django. Are you sure it's installed and "
            "available on your PYTHONPATH environment variable? Did you "
            "forget to activate a virtual environment?"
        ) from exc
    execute_from_command_line(sys.argv)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
